# NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) Mathematics - Continuity and Differentiability

## Topic 1: Continuity

**Total Questions:** 74

---

### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider a function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>≥</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> If <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous as well as differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous and differentiable <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> must be continuous and differentiable <math><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> therefore, it is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> also.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> … <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Also, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>K</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>K</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math>
For <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> to be finite
<math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
So, <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>. The value of <math> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>&#960;</mi> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math> is continuous at <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mi>&#960;</mi> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msqrt><mi>e</mi></msqrt></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msqrt><mi>e</mi></msqrt></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>cot</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>cos</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>cot</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mi>e</mi><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></munder><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>c</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math></p><p>and <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math> is equal to</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>as</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>,</mo></math> where <math><mi>p</mi></math> is a real number. If <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>p</mi></math> cannot be (where <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> represents the greatest integer function)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
If <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math> should be an integer.</p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math> cannot be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo>:</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi>n </mi><mi>x </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi>s </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi>l </mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo>:</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi>n </mi><mi>x </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi>s </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous for all real <math><mi>x</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous for all real <math><mi>x</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous only at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous only at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous only at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi>a</mi></math> be any real number.
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> (when <math><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></math> through rational values)
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></math> (when <math><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></math> through irrational values)
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> will exist only when <math><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
i.e., when <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
Thus, if <math><mi>a</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>1</mn></math> or <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> then <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> will not exist
Hence, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> will be discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> where <math><mi>a</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>1</mn></math> or <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** discontinuous at exactly two points
- **(B)** differentiable <math><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(C)** differentiable <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** continuous <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>-</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) differentiable <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
 <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math></p><p><math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
For differentiability,
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable columnalign="left" equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr columnalign="left"> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr columnalign="left"> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr columnalign="left"> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd columnalign="left"> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>non-differentiable
at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo></math>non-differentiable</p>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>log</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>π</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mi>λ</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>λ</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

For the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></math>

<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

Now, <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi></math>

Putting <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi></math>, we get, 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi></math>

<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> defined as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **63.75**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Since, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> so, at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> both left hand and right hand limits must exist and both must be equal to <math><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
Now,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
If <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> exists, then <math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The function <math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="}" open="{" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext> sin </mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>π</mi> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>, where [.] denotes the greatest integer function and {.} is the fractional part function, is discontinuous at

**Options:**

- **(A)** all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
- **(B)** all integer points
- **(C)** no <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> which is not an integer

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) no <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="}" open="{" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>&#160;sin&#160;</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>&#960;</mi> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="}"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mi>sin</mi></math> (integral multiple of <math> <mrow> <mi>&#960;</mi> </mrow> </math>)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous for all <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>.

</details>

---

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#945;</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous and differentiable at any point, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>, </mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The given function is clearly continuous at all points except possibly at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
For <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we must have
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>α</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> … <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, for <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we must have
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>α</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∵</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>∴</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>α</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>α</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Putting <math><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> in <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> we get <math><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If f(x) = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>λ</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>μ</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>12,</mn><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>9</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>μ</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>L</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mi>&#955;</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>12</mn></math> <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>+4</mo></math> ......(1)

Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> <math><mi>L</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>D</mi></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#183;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi></math> ......(2)

By (1) and (2)</p><p><math><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mo>&#183;</mo></mfenced></math>denotes the greatest integer function. If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** No value is possible

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> wil make <math><mi>f</mi></math> continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>c</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> for some constants <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>50</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **18.75**

**Detailed Solution:**

In <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> for <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> form <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
Now, <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
For <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> (where, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integer function). Then, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mo>′</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** does not exist
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) does not exist**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, LHL of <math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mo>&#x2032;</mo>  </msup>  <mfenced>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder>   <mrow>    <mi>lim</mi></mrow>   <mrow>    <mi>h</mi><mo>&#x2192;</mo><msup>     <mn>0</mn>     <mo>+</mo>    </msup>    </mrow>  </munder>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced><mfenced>     <mrow>      <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow>    </mfenced><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced><mfenced>     <mrow>      <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>→</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∞</mi></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x2234;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mo>&#x2032;</mo>  </msup>  <mfenced>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math> does not exist.

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math>, then the value of<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>12</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> , therefore,

<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>∞</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>∞</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math>

</details>

### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>≤</mo><mn>12</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> If <math><mi>S</mi></math> is the set of points in the interval <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> at which <math><mi>f</mi></math> is not differentiable, then <math><mi>S</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** an empty set
- **(D)** equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) equal to <math><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>12</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>12</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/c0237cb5-44dc-4cf5-a8f5-3a7c13ce3248.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.97361in;height:1.37986in" />
Hence, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>g</mi><mi> </mi></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>g</mi></math>  is not differentiable at  <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In the neighborhood of <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>′</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>–</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> <math><msup><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>′</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>
