### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left" columnspacing="1.4ex"><mtr><mtd><msup><mtext>x</mtext><mrow><mtext>p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mtext>:x</mtext><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mtext>:x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> then at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> the function <math><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>x</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** Continuous if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** Continuous if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** Continuous and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Continuous if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and differentiable if <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Continuity at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">L</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">L</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>f&#160;p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">R</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">L</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>f&#160;p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
and <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0&#160;</mn><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">f&#160;</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Differentiability at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>:
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>L.H.D.</mtext><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> if <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>R.H.D.</mtext><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mtext>h</mtext><mrow><mtext>p</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mtext>h</mtext></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">l</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">i</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">cos</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">h</mtext></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> if <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">f</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> if <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">p</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"><mtable columnalign="left" columnspacing="1.4ex"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>. At <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mtext>g</mtext></math> is differentiable but <math> <msup>  <mtext>g</mtext>  <mo>&#x2032;</mo> </msup> </math> is not continuous
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable while <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></math> is not differentiable
- **(C)** both <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> are non differentiable
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable and <math> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>′</mo> </msup> </math> is continuous

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable and <math> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>′</mo> </msup> </math> is continuous**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We have, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
For <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mtext>x</mtext> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mi>cos</mi><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mfrac> <mfenced> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>sin</mi><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext>g</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mtext>x</mtext><mn>3</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><msup><mtext>g</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><msup><mtext>x</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mtext>x</mtext></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math> <msup> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> </math> is continuous at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Also, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> .</p>

</details>

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1,</mn></math> then <math><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></math> can be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>π</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi>π</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Also <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> must be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
Now for <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> or <math><mn>0</mn><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From equation <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, we get
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8801;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, <math><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#960;</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is (where, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integer function and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>N</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> but discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> but continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> but continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,1</mo></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8713;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
For <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>. Then, in the interval <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>g</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** not differentiable at one point
- **(B)** differentiable at all points
- **(C)** not continuous
- **(D)** not differentiable at two points

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) not differentiable at one point**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
And <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2,2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>g</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> <mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mspace linebreak="newline"/><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><msup><mi>g</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
So, <math><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the incorrect statement is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> both are continuous <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Now, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>1,2</mn></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>1,2</mn></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnalign="left" equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd/></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd/></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Clearly, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mtext>'</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
and <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is non-differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is not defined at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></math>. The value of <math><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>)</mo></math>, so that <math><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munder><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>cos</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>cos</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munder><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>tan</mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>tan</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munder><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

Since, <math><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munder><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>{</mo><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mtext>    0</mtext><mo>≤</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>sgn</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mtext>    </mtext><mn>1</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></math>

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></math> is differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1,</mn></math> then the value of <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>|</mo> <mrow> <mi>a</mi><mo>−</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow> <mo>|</mo></mrow></mrow> </math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></math>

For continuity at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>i</mi></mfenced></math>

In <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

In <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></math>

For differentiability at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> . This with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>i</mi><mo>)</mo></math> gives the values <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>−</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a continuous function such that its value <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math> is a rational number and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Since <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous and attains only rational values, then by intermediate value theorem, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is a constant function.</p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi></math>, then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** non differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** non differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) non differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>1,5</mn></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> both are continuous<math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is also continuous <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Now,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Now, check differentiability
At <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></math>
<math><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>at</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>differentiable</mi></math> 
At <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>125</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>10</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>125</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>10</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </math>
Clearly, <math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> non-differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> (where, <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mo>&#8901;</mo> </mfenced></mrow> </math> denotes the greatest integer function and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>N</mi></math>) is

**Options:**

- **(A)** continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> but discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> but continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8713;</mo><mi>I</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>I</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
For <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mn>1</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
For <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable everywhere, then <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mn>9</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is also differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> &amp; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, we get,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>16</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></math> is not defined at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math> The value of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> such that <math><mi>f</mi></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mi>e</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi>e</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi>e</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mi>f</mi></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> if
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mi>e</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the points of discontinuity of the function <math><msup><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> where <math><msup><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi></math> (<math><mi>n</mi></math> times) are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2,1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1,2</mn></math>
- **(D)** no points of discontinuity

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Clearly, <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is a point of discontinuity of the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>.
if <math><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> which is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>.
If <math><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mo> </mo><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi></math>
Which is continuous everywhere.
Hence, <math><msup><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo></math> which is continuous everywhere.
So, the only points of discontinuity are <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** continuous and differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** continuous but not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** differentiable but not continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) None of these**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>For function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
L.H.L. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
R.H.L. <math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Since, <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math></p><p>therefore, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not continuous and not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math></p>

</details>
