### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></math> so that the function <math> <mrow> <mtext>f</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> is continuous everywhere is <math><mtext>k</mtext></math>, then value of <math> <mrow> <mn>10</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>k</mtext></mrow> </math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1.25**

**Detailed Solution:**

If <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mrow><mo>=</mo> <munder> <mtext>lim</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>→</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </munder> <mtext>f</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

      <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <munder> <mtext>lim</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>→</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

      <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <munder> <mtext>lim</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>→</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msup> <mo>·</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>·</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

      <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <munder> <mtext>lim</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>→</mo> <mn>0 </mn> </mrow> </munder> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>×</mo> <munder> <mtext>lim</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>→</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </munder> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>×</mo> <munder> <mtext>lim</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>→</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos </mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

      <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>×</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext>k</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>8</mn> </mfrac> <mo>⇒</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>k</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>5</mn> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>1.25</mn></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the function  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable><mtr><mtd><mtext>x</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>a</mtext><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mtext>&#160;sin&#160;x</mtext></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mtext>x&#160;cot&#160;x</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>b</mtext></mtd><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8804;</mo><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mtext>a&#160;cos&#160;</mtext><mn>2</mn><mtext>x</mtext><mo>-</mo><mtext>b&#160;sin&#160;x&#160;&#160;</mtext></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is continuous in <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>,&#160;</mtext> <mi>&#960;</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>, then the values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></math> respectively are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mtext>,&#160;</mtext><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mtext>,&#160;</mtext><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8757;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo> <mtext>f</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math> is continuous in <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>,&#160;</mtext> <mi>&#960;</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math> so it is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mtext>and&#160;&#160;</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mtext>a</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mtext>b</mtext></math>          .....(1)

and   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mtext>x</mtext><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mtext>f</mtext><mfenced><mtext>x</mtext></mfenced></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>b</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>b</mtext> </mrow> </math>                 .....(2)

By solving (1) and (2), we get, 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo><mtext>a</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mtext>,&#160;</mtext><mtext>b</mtext><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd></mtr></mtable><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac bevelled="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></math> is continuous at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **30**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><msup><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac><mfrac><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mn>1.3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math>

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">∞</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous and differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but not differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at infinite number of points.
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at two points.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous and differentiable for all <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Clearly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous and differentiable <math><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

- **(D)** <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>−</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>−</mo><mfenced><mi>b</mi></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>−</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>−</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced><mtext>i</mtext></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mtext>i</mtext></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∵</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi></math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder> <mrow> <mtext>lim</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mn>0</mn> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&#8658;</mo><munder> <mrow> <mtext>lim</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </munder> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow> </mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced> <mi>b</mi> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mn>0</mn></mfenced></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><munder><mtext>lim</mtext><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>h</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mi>h</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo></msup></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math>

Then, the values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We have,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mi>l</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mi>l</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math></p><p>Also, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi></math>
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we must have
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>l</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>l</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the points of discontinuity of the function <math><msup><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> where <math><msup><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi></math> (<math><mi>n</mi></math> times), are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1,2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** continuous everywhere except <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) continuous everywhere except <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Clearly, <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is a point of discontinuity of the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> .
If <math><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>1</mn></math> , then
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi></math> , which is continuous <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math> .
If <math><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>1</mn></math> , then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><msup><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi><mi></mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> which is continuous everywhere except <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, the only point of discontinuity is <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></math>,
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math>
Putting <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi></math>, we get,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>tan</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math>
So, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mrow><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,&#160;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous and differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but non differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous but differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous and non differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but non differentiable at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let us shift the graph of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> in the direction of the negative <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis by <math><mn>4</mn></math> units
&amp; let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> will have the same behaviour as of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>tan</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfrac></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
Continuity at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>,
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac bevelled="true"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>π</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></munder><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
Put <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> such that the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mroot><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mroot><mo>-</mo><mroot><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mroot></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>For <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we must have
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mroot><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mroot><mo>-</mo><mroot><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mroot></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>12</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>32</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mi>x</mi></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>12</mn></mfrac></math></p>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable everywhere,</p><p>then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5.85**

**Detailed Solution:**

Continuity at<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>p</mi></math>
Continuity at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>9</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>,
then value of  <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **12**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
for numerator to be zero <math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi></mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
now after rationalization,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
again on rationalization,</p><p><math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnspacing="1.4ex" columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>q</mi></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable everywhere, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>71</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>51</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>33</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>71</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> Continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mi>q</mi></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msqrt><mi>q</mi></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math>
Continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>9</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>ii</mi></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mtable><mtr/><mtr/></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> Differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>2</mn></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>iii</mi></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> Differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mn>3</mn></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>6</mn><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi>iv</mi></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math> {from equations <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>}
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>p</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>q</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>71</mn><mn>10</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left" columnspacing="1.4ex"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** Continuous as well as differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** Continuous but not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** Differentiable but not continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) None of these**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mo>⁡</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> therefore, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not continuous and not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></math> for <math><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math> Then value of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> which make <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>⋅</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>
