### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mo>:</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>R</mi></math> is a function defined by <math><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo></math> where <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integer function, then <math><mi>f</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** continuous for every real <math><mi>x</mi></math>
- **(B)** discontinuous only at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** discontinuous only at integral values of <math><mi>x</mi></math>
- **(D)** continuous only at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) discontinuous only at integral values of <math><mi>x</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Doubtful points are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mi>&#960;</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mi>&#960;</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>n</mi></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mi>n</mi></math> 
Clearly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>LHL</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>RHL</mi><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> all cannot be equal for any <math><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi></math>
Hence, discontinuous at all integeral values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>

</details>

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let the function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is continuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>. Then, the value of the function at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** indeterminate

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> we must have <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>×</mo><mi>a</mi></math> finite quantity <math><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

---

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfrac><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></msqrt><mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>. If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2.40**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></msqrt></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
For the denominator to be zero, <math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
Now, on rationalization
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></msqrt><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi></msqrt></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which of the following is a correct statement?

**Options:**

- **(A)** Continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
- **(B)** Differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
- **(C)** Existence of limit at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
- **(D)** Differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is necessary for existence of tangent at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) Differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>, then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is also continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
Hence, differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is sufficient for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </msqrt> <mo>−</mo><mo> </mo><msqrt> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mo>−</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> <mi>x</mi> </mfrac> <mo>,</mo></mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>,</mo></mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo> </mo><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is continuous in <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mo>],</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math> then the value of <math> <mi>a</mi> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given that <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous in the interval <math><mo>[</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn><mo>]</mo></math>, so, it will be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>.

Now <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">x </mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mo>(</mo><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mi> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi> </mi><msqrt><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mi> </mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> (rationalizing)

<math><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

Now <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo></math> (<math><mo>∵</mo></math> <math><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is continuous at point 0)

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> <math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> (where <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> denotes the greatest integer function), then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>LHL</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mi>RHL</mi></math> at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>f</mi><mi></mi><mo>:</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>R</mi></math> be a function defined as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mo> </mo></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>15</mn></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>. Then, <math><mi>f</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** continuous in <math><mi>R</mi></math> if <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** continuous in <math><mi>R</mi></math> if <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** continuous in <math><mi>R</mi></math> if <math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** not continuous in <math><mi>R</mi></math> for any values of <math><mi>a</mi></math> and <math><mi>b</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) not continuous in <math><mi>R</mi></math> for any values of <math><mi>a</mi></math> and <math><mi>b</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>15</mn></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Also, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>15</mn><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Also, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> , clearly, <math><mi>f</mi></math> is not continuous for any values of <math><mi>a</mi></math> and <math><mi>b</mi></math> .

</details>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>p</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>r</mi><mi>sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is continuous in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the number of points at which <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable is/are

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

At <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>p</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
At <math><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
At <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> <math><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>r</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>N</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>N</mi><mi>D</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not differentiable at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mo>∀</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mn>0,10</mn></mfenced></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mo>·</mo></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mo>·</mo></mfenced></math> are the greatest integer and fractional part functions respectively). The number of points of discontinuity of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
Hence, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous <math><mo>∀</mo><mi></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></math> upto <math><mi>∞,</mi></math> then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> does not exist
- **(B)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous but not differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><munder><mi>lim</mi><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math> (as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>)
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8800;</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>tan</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mi>x</mi></mfrac><mo> </mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>k</mi></mtd><mtd><mo> </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>, then <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> for

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** no values of <math><mi>k</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) no values of <math><mi>k</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
and <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>≠</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> for any value of <math><mi>k</mi></math>

</details>

### Q63. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>b</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is differentiable <math><mo>∀</mo><mi></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> then <math><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>240</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>85</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>250</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> must be continuous and differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
For continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>a</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
For differentiability at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>a</mi> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><msqrt> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mi>b</mi> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>;</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mrow> <mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>b</mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>18</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q64. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>,</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> then (where, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></math> represents the greatest integer function)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
And, <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Since, <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>≠</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo></math> therefore, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is not continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q65. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is differentiable,  it must be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> ... <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>f</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close=""><mtable columnalign="left" equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></msqrt></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>

Now, for <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be differentiable at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#8722;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <msup> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> ... <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

From equation <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> &amp; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q66. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>x</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>:</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi></math> are arbitrary constants) is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to</p><p>(use <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>e</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>.7</mo></math>)</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **7.29**

**Detailed Solution:**

At <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>LHL</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>RHL</mi><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>h</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>e</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>h</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>e</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>h</mi></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>e</mi></math>
and <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>e</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>e</mi></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q67. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>N</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> only
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> only
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,1</mn></math> and <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8734;</mi></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfenced><mi>n</mi></msup></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd></mtr></mtable><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8804;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="{" close="" separators="|"><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
Thus, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is discontinuous at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1,3</mn></math>

### Q68. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>9</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **7.20**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to be continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>.</mo></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn></math>
for the above limit to satisfy
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>,
<math><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and 
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>8</mn></math>

</details>

### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, such that <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> can be made continuous at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> is

- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

<p><math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math> <math><mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">lim</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi </mi></mrow></mrow></math>

Thus, for continuity at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of discontinuity of the greatest integer function <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** 104
- **(B)** 102
- **(C)** 101
- **(D)** 103

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 103**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3.5</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

As we know greatest integer is discontinuous on integer values.

In given interval, the integer values are

<math><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mo>…</mo><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mn>99</mn><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Total numbers of integers are 103.

</details>
